- Owner of the sums and the payback: `computations/cost-lines.js` (loaded by energy-department.html after fan-savings.js). `measureCostTotals(m, laborRate)`, `sumCostTotals`, `costLineAmount` and `simplePayback(gross, incentive, annual)` = (total cost - incentive) / annual savings $. It is the ONLY payback formula. `years` is null when there is no cost or no savings; 0 when the incentive is larger than the cost.
- Labor rate: the pricing estimator hourly rate, `_pricingGetConfig().hourlyRate` (app/pricing-estimator.js; default `COST_LABOR_RATE_DEFAULT` 170 $/hr; set in the Cost Estimate toolbar). `msrLaborRate()` (app/energy-savings.js) reads it when the cost is shown. A labor line stores hours only, never a rate or a dollar amount.
- Savings side: `msrAnnualSavings(projId, m)` (app/energy-savings.js) = `calcMeasureDollars` annual total at the measure rates (`measureRatesNow`). `getProjMatrixSavingsTotal` calls it. `msrCostSummary(projId, m)` (one measure) and `projCostSummary(projId, measures)` (roll-up) call `simplePayback`. The roll-up payback divides by the matrix savings of the measures that have a cost (a measure with no cost line adds savings but no cost); `annualAll` is the savings of all measures in the list.
- Where it shows: the Financials block of the measure detail on the Energy Savings tab (`msrFinancialsHtml`: lines, total cost, incentive, net cost, matrix savings, payback); the two items "Net Cost" and "Simple Payback" of the Selected Measures card (`_svCostItemsHtml`); the Cost Estimate tab roll-up table (`_pricingMeasureCostRollupHTML`, app/pricing-estimator.js, above the tier table in every estimate type) and the footer savings line (`_pricingSavingsLineHTML`). The pricing estimator does no energy math: it reads cost lines and matrix savings.
- Fan Savings: `ecmAddAsMeasure` adds `defaultCostLinesFor('fan_savings')`: one internal labor line "Reprogram BAS for variable speed" with NO hours (the labor model, `COST_PER_SEQ_HOURS_DEFAULT`, has no task for this work, so no hours are invented). The user types the hours.
- Removed payback copies: the two Financials panels and the Selected Measures summary of `renderSavingsMatrix` / `renderSavingsSummary` (those functions drew into ids no page has; deleted with `renderSavingsFooter`), the Solar Calculator system cost, tax credit and payback (app/calculators.js), `o.paybackYrs` and `o.installCost` of the Woodland report data (app/report-engine-woodland.js).
- Escaping: vendor, description, note, measure name and measure notes go through `_escHtml`. The handlers read ids from data attributes (`data-proj`, `data-msr`, `data-line`), so no id is put in a script string.
- Data move (2026-10-14): `migrateOldCostFieldsToLines` (app/energy-savings.js) runs at the end of `loadUtilityData` (app/utility-data.js), on every page load. For each saved measure it calls `moveOldCostFieldsToLines` (computations/cost-lines.js): an old `implCost` above 0 becomes a third-party line "Implementation cost (moved from old field)", an old `incentive` above 0 becomes an incentive line, and both old fields are deleted. A measure that already has cost lines keeps them. It saves `en_projects` once, only when a measure changed, and writes the console line `[cost field move]`. A second run finds no old field and does nothing. Check: load a backup that has a measure with `implCost`, then open the Financials block; `node tools/test-cost-lines.js` section 8.
- Runs: in the browser on render and on each edit. No timer, no network, no env var, no schedule.
- Check: `node tools/test-cost-lines.js` (totals, payback, fan line, gate-fails-on-a-copy, hostile-text render, solarCostLines, old-field data move) and `node tools/gate-single-payback.js` (in the regression gate static group): fails on a second definition of `simplePayback`, any code identifier with "payback" in it, the retired `implCost` and `installCost`, or a measure `.incentive`.

## Modal footer and bill flag reasons (2026-10-14)

- Shared modal structure: a window uses `.modal` > `.modal-hdr` + `.modal-body` + `.modal-ftr` (energy-department.html CSS, `.modal` is a flex column, only `.modal-body` scrolls). A footer inside the body scrolls out of view, so do not put one there. Bills Table Settings, Export Utility Data (app/utility-data.js), Budget line and Hours entry (app/budget.js, app/hours.js), BAS alarm and trend import (app/bas-alarms.js, app/bas-trends.js) now use it.
- `billFlagReasonList(flags)` (computations/bill-flags.js) is the one list of flag reason texts. The Bills table row hover and the banner (`#billFlagBanner`, `renderBillsPane` in app/utility-data.js) both call it. The banner and the row hover now both call `billFlagLine(label, flags)` (computations/bill-flags.js): one plain sentence per bill, several reasons as short clauses joined with "; " plus one action sentence. `billFlagLabel(bill, allBills, index0)` gives the month, or "Bill N" (the # column row number) when the bill has no dates. A bill with no dates and blank usage but a charge reads: "Bill 1 has no dates or usage, but it has a $7,398.79 gas charge. Open the bill to add the dates and usage, or delete it." Rule sentences ("Flag at ...") are no longer in any message; they are in `BILL_FLAG_RULES[rule].rule`. Which bills are flagged and the counts did not change. How to check: open a meter with a flagged bill on the Bills tab; `node tools/test-bill-flag-rules.js` section 10.

## Flagged-bill badges (2026-10-14)

- Declutter (2026-10-14, backlog fm-20261014-br1): a warning or count shows ONLY for a problem that changes usage, kW, cost, billing dates, a missing bill or the baseline. `BILL_COUNTED_FLAG_RULES` (computations/bill-flags.js) is the ONE list of counted flag types: dates_missing, period_length, period_overlap, period_gap, duplicate_bill, charge_sum_mismatch, printed_read_mismatch (the rule only emits it when the kWh was computed from reads that disagree), usage_missing, usage_negative, cost_missing, charge_vs_usage, usage_charge_mismatch. Facilities kW is not a flag any more (2026-10-15): it changes no cost, so a blank facKW gives no warning, and the old stored `facKWMissing_warn` entry is neither written nor read. A "usage is blank" message names no read-difference number (the gas table has no such column). NOT counted: usage_vs_same_month, water_sewer_parity, days_mismatch, cost_field_mismatch, read_continuity, read_rollback, read_usage_mismatch. `computeMeterFlagSummary` returns `perBill` (every flag: the bill row's own hover and dots, and the Review Bill Corrections list) and `countedPerBill` / `flaggedBills` / `flagCount` (counted types only: meter pill, building row, project rows, the Bills banner and its reason list). `computeBuildingFlagSummary(building, isExcluded)` and `computeProjectFlagSummary(buildings, isExcluded)` skip a meter that `isBaselineExcluded(projId, m.id)` names (callers: renderUDProjList, renderProjUDBldgNav in app/core.js, renderSidebarFolders in app/energy-savings.js); the excluded meter's own pill still shows its own count. Hover on every flag badge: "N bills with problems that change usage, cost or dates" (`billFlagHoverText`). The sidebar orange `.spfi-badge` hover reads "N open tasks".
- Meter pill baseline chip: `udMeterBaselineChipHtml(projId, m)` (app/utility-data.js), one plain chip per state, no warning symbol: "Not in baseline" (excluded by the user), "Baseline locked", "Baseline not locked", "No weather in baseline", "Baseline not set" (states from `getBaselineTrustState`), each with a one-sentence hover. Building row: `udBuildingBaselineChipHtml(projId, b)` ("2 of 2 baselined": meters that count in the baseline and have baseline months saved); it is the only copy (the second copy in app/core.js is deleted). The A-F data quality letter is gone from the meter pill; `computeMeterQualityScore` (computations/data-quality.js) still feeds the meter Stats pane card.
- A read the load-time rule corrected is not marked in the Bills table (no tint, no hover, no banner). The Edit Billing Period window lists it in a "Change history" section at the bottom (`_billChangeHistory` in app/utility-data.js, built on `_readCorrectionRecords`; also lists `_userCorrected` edits; hidden when the bill has none); the `_auto_corrected_*` data stays on the bill.
- One count, three levels: `computeMeterFlagSummary` -> `computeBuildingFlagSummary` -> `computeProjectFlagSummary(buildings)` (computations/bill-flags.js). The Bills banner, the meter pill, the building row and the project row in the Utility Data list (left) all show `flaggedBills` from them. `udFlagBadgeHtml(summary, goto)` (app/utility-data.js) is the one badge builder (hover text from `billFlagHoverText`). Shown only when the count is above 0.
- Same badge in two more places (builder and summary functions unchanged): the sidebar Projects list (`renderSidebarFolders`, app/energy-savings.js; the orange `.spfi-badge` next to it is the open task count, a different number) and the building rows of a project's Utility Data tab (`renderProjUDBldgNav`, app/core.js). Their badges pass `embed: true`, so `udGoToFlagged(..., true)` opens the project, the Utility Data tab, the building and the first flagged meter's Bills tab. The sidebar is drawn again after `initUtilityTool` (app/core.js) because the bills load there.
- Click on a building or project badge calls `udGoToFlagged` (app/utility-data.js): opens the first flagged meter on the Bills tab. No timer, no env var, no server call.
- Live update: `saveUtilityData` ends with `udScheduleBadgeRefresh` (one `setTimeout(0)`; it draws `renderUDProjList`, `renderSidebarFolders` and every `renderProjUDBldgNav` again), so edit, import and delete change the badges without a reload.
- Meter pills too: `udScheduleBadgeRefresh` also calls `udRefreshMeterPills` (app/utility-data.js), which draws the pills of the selected building again in the standalone view and in each open project tab. `udMeterPillsHtml` is the one pill builder (`renderUDDetail` calls it too).
- Cache: `computeMeterFlagSummary` keeps one result per meter object (WeakMap in computations/bill-flags.js), used only when the signature of the meter's commodity, inclusive flag and bills (and, for water and sewer, the partner meters' bills) is unchanged. No bill write path needs to clear it. `_computeMeterFlagSummaryFresh` is the uncached run, used by the gate. Callers must not change the returned object. On 10 projects x 10 buildings x 5 meters x 60 bills, a refresh after one bill edit took about 15 s before and about 60 ms after; the first cold pass on page load still takes about 5-7 s.
- Project names in the sidebar list and the Utility Data project list pass through `_escHtml` (after the 22-character cut).
- Check: `node tools/gate-single-bill-flag-count.js` (runs `tools/gate-bill-flag-behavior.js`); open a project with flagged bills, fix one, watch the numbers drop.

## Meter read repair (2026-10-14)

- What: one rule repairs a start read, end read or read difference the PDF reader misread, using the previous bill's end read and the next bill's start read on the same meter. `computeReadCorrection` (computations/bill-flags.js, search the name). Evidence principle: a value is evidence only when the check that confirms it would fail for every 1- or 2-digit misread of that value. Reads compare within one unit of the coarsest last printed digit (print rounding can make two printed values that should agree differ by one unit; the read_continuity tolerance is not used), and a correction must move a field by more than that one unit, so a one-unit difference is never changed. The read difference D is proven by the usage (`_rcUsageProves`) only when |D x M - U| <= half a unit of the usage's last printed digit AND that residual plus half a usage unit is smaller than one unit of D's last printed digit times M. D and U are NOT independent: the PDF reader rewrites D = E - S and U = D x M, and a misread can store D and U both misread but consistent. So a change of the end read (c) or of the read difference (d) needs the next bill's start read; without it both decline. On-peak + off-peak kWh are not evidence (their sum differs from the billed kWh by up to about 1 kWh on actual bills; one D step is 0.016 kWh at multiplier 160). Cases, D proven: a, both reads off by the same amount, next bill's start = previous end + D; a0, the same on the NEWEST bill (no next bill), provisional: identical offset in both reads, each a 1- or 2-digit misread, start read >= 10 x D (a replaced meter starts near zero), the previous bill clean (its span = its D, its D proven by its usage) and the shift DOWN past the previous bill's start read (otherwise the previous bill's end read could be the misread; a shift up or a smaller shift waits for the next bill); b, only the start wrong (end - previous end = D; next start, if present, = end); c, only the end wrong (start = previous end, next start = start + D). D not proven: d, start = previous end and next start = end, so the READ DIFFERENCE becomes end - start (must agree with the usage within half a unit). Any other mix, a read difference printed coarser than the reads, a bill without the needed next bill: no change, the flag stays. A value changes only when the new and old text have the same length and differ in at most 2 characters. Self-check (`_rcRevertProvisional`): on every walk, an a0 correction whose bill now has a linked next bill with a start read is kept (its records become rule-a records) when that start read equals the corrected end read and reverted (reader values back from the records, records removed, read_continuity flag on) when it does not. A bill links to the previous one only when it starts 0 to GAP_DAYS days after it (a duplicate or overlapping bill never links); extracted bills are walked in date order. A meter change, a rollover, a multiplier change, an estimated bill or a gap is never changed. User wins: a bill with a corrected field the user changed afterwards, or a field in `_userCorrected`, is skipped; every edit path (saveBillRow, submitValueCorrection, updateBillField in app/csv-import.js, the Review Bill Corrections apply in app/bill-corrections-review.js, both stores, single and bulk) calls `clearReadCorrectionRecords`, which removes the record and stamps `_userCorrected`.
- Callers: `correctMeterReadChain(meter)` for every meter in `loadUtilityData` (app/utility-data.js, runs on every page load, in the user's browser, no time zone, no timer). It saves through `saveUtilityData(SAVE_ALL_PROJECTS)` only when it changed a bill, and a second load changes nothing. `correctExtractedReadChain` in the PDF read-chain step (app/bill-analysis.js, runs per PDF with 2 or more bills). `readCorrectionMarkers` copies the record to every saved-bill builder.
- Original kept: `_auto_corrected_startRead` / `_auto_corrected_endRead` / `_auto_corrected_readDifference` = `{ original, corrected, reason }` on the stored bill (capitalized names on an extracted bill). The Bills table does not mark the cell; the record shows in the Edit Billing Period "Change history" section (since 2026-10-14 there is no banner, no diamond mark and no cell tint; the flag reason list `.bill-banner-list` is capped at 120px with its own scroll in energy-department.html). A record whose corrected value no longer matches the cell is not drawn.
- Speed: `_rcWalk` (computations/bill-flags.js) skips a bill that has no correction record and whose start read equals the previous end read and whose end read minus start read equals its read difference, both within print rounding (`_rcCleanLink`); each read is parsed once per walk. Measured with `500 meters x 60 bills`: about 450 ms before, about 100 ms after, per load.
- Needs: no env var, no network, no schedule. Console line on a repair: `[read-chain correction] Repaired the meter reads of N bill(s)`.
- Check: `node tools/gate-read-chain-correction.js` (about 40 s; in the regression gate). Property tests over 9 multipliers, 4 read precisions, 5 usage precisions, with and without a next bill: every 1- and 2-digit substitution of every read field (about 1.5 million cases), both reads shifted by the same misread offset (about 620,000), the read difference misread with the usage recomputed from it (about 307,000; the real correlated bill), and about 1.6 million real meter replacements with no next bill (a replacement that starts under ten read differences is never changed; refurbished-meter coincidences at or above are counted and printed, the self-check reverts them). It fails if a correct field is ever changed or a changed field is not the true value; plus the scenario cases (a, a0, b, c, d, the previous bill's end read misread with its D and usage rewritten, one-unit print rounding, coarse usage, two culprits, meter replacement, multiplier change, duplicate, out of order, estimated, gap, overlap, rollover, user edit, edit-path clear, extraction order, self-check confirm and contradict, banner markup and CSS). To check by eye: open a meter with a repaired bill on the Bills tab and click the mark.

## Printed read check (2026-10-14)

- What: the PDF reader keeps a printed read difference and a printed kWh exactly as extracted (app/bill-analysis.js, Step 1 and Step 2 of the read chain, and the recovery pass). It computes end read minus start read, or read difference times multiplier, only when the PDF printed no value. The arithmetic is a CHECK: `computePrintedReadMismatch` (computations/bill-flags.js, the only copy). Tolerance is the print precision: reads and the difference may differ by one unit of the coarsest last digit; the kWh may differ from difference times multiplier by half a unit of its last digit plus half a unit of the difference's last digit times the multiplier, plus (when the multiplier prints decimals) half a unit of the multiplier's last digit times the difference. Decimals are counted on the number only (`12.00CR`, `(12.00)` and `12.00 kWh` have 2). A rollover (end under start) is checked only when the reader saw the meter's dial limit (`b._meterRollover.boundary`, set by the Step 1 rollover branch whether or not the difference was printed): the expected difference is limit + 1 - start + end. The rollover branch keeps a printed difference and computes only a missing one.
- Where it is recorded: `readCorrectionMarkers` copies `_printedReadCheck = { fields, messages, kwhComputed, limit? }` to every saved bill that has a mismatch (data only, no warning, in no badge count and not in billFlagReasonList). `clearReadCorrectionRecords` removes it when the kWh is edited and computes it again from the stored values when a read is edited; `_rcApply` (read-chain repair) does the same through `_rcRefreshPrintedCheck`, which drops the record when nothing disagrees any more.
- The one visible flag: rule `printed_read_mismatch` in computeMeterFlagSummary. It shows only when the bill has `_printedReadCheck.kwhComputed` (the PDF printed no kWh) AND the stored reads still disagree with the stored read difference, so the usage itself is in doubt. The check runs again on the stored values, so a corrected or edited read clears the flag.
- Needs: no env var, no network, no schedule, runs in the user's browser on extraction and on every flag count.
- Row tint: a bill row in Utility Data (app/utility-data.js, row render) gets the amber tint and the dots only when the bill has a flag in `BILL_COUNTED_FLAG_RULES`; a bill whose flags are all outside that list shows no warning text. The row hover is `billRowHoverText(label, flags)` (computations/bill-flags.js): the counted flags only, through `billFlagLine`, so it reads the same as the banner. The Bills table cells format numbers with `_billFormatValue` (app/csv-import.js), which calls `parseBillNumber`, so a stored "7,398.79" shows $7,398.79. Check: `node tools/gate-bill-display-and-hover.js`. The Review Bill Corrections panel still lists every flagged bill.
- Missing kWh (2026-10-14): `_decideQuantityCorrection` (app/bill-analysis.js) treats a missing current kWh as missing. It used to call `toFixed` on it and throw, which skipped Step 1/2 and the recovery pass. A self-verified witness fills it; weak witnesses alone change nothing and leave `kWhConsumed` unlocked, so Step 2 (or the recovery pass) computes it from read difference times multiplier.
- Check: `node tools/gate-printed-read-mismatch.js` (synthetic values; it runs `_postExtractionVerify` on synthetic bills; in the regression gate static list). Bills saved before this change hold no `_printedReadCheck`, so they never show this flag.

## Utility E detached amounts, label repair and "propose, never fill" (2026-10-15)

- Where: the Utility E extract function in app/energy-savings.js and its helper just above it: `_evgReadDetachedAmounts`. Runs in the user's browser when a bill PDF is read (also the auto-repair pass). No schedule, no env var, no network.
- Charge labels: `_EVG_KW` (inside the extract function) is the one map of charge-label patterns; `xChg`, `xRate`, the detached-amount label count and the Off Pk Sum label repair all read it.
- Amount readers: the Tax exempt delivery, Franchise Fee and Sales Tax readers take dollar amounts only from `_evgChargeAmounts`, so a rate on the next line ("14,397.6017 kWh at $0.07852 per kWh") is never cut to "$0.07".
- Never filled, always proposed: a charge whose printed amount was not read is NOT stored from qty x rate (one line, or a multi-line sum that needs qty x rate for any line); a total is NOT stored as the sum of the charges when the reader found no printed Subtotal / Current Charges (extract function and `_postExtractionVerify` Stage 3a in app/bill-analysis.js). Each writes `_proposed_<field> = { value, reason, ... }`; `_applyExtractionGates` (app/bill-analysis.js) turns every `_proposed_*` into a hold reason "<field> ... gives $x — NOT applied". A proposal is dropped when a later printed-value rule fills the field (gap closed to the cent against the printed total, detached amount). The Tax exempt / Bill offset mirror still fills the missing side (the two lines cancel, no cost changes) and marks it `_mirror_filled_<field>`; no hold.
- Detached amount column (`_evgReadDetachedAmounts`): when OCR puts the dollar amounts in a block of 3 or more amount-only lines, each amount goes to the one charge line whose qty x rate (x printed day proration) equals it to the cent, one to one. A charge is stored only when every one of its lines matched and the page shows no more label lines than parts; ECA/EER/PTS segments must add up to kWhConsumed, On Pk + Off Pk together too. The one negative amount is the Bill offset and its positive twin the Tax exempt delivery (both labels present without an amount). The Customer charge is the first amount only when the block has one amount per label and every other amount matched. Record: `_detached_amount_<field>`.
- Off Pk Sum label repair (changed 2026-10-23): `_evgRepairOffPkSumLine` is deleted. A lost Energy label is recovered by `touResolveLines` (computations/rates.js) only, with the same proof (the four kWh add up to kWh used). Record: `_touLines.labelRecovered` (and `_touLines.reconciled`).
- On/Off peak kWh from a charge line: the qty is copied to OnPeakKWh / OffPeakKWh only when that leg's dollar charge was read.
- Rate schedule: the header read tolerates the OCR stutter "Billing De Details".
- Check: `node tools/test-utility-e-detached-amounts.js` (synthetic pages; in the regression gate's tools/test-*.js list; expect ALL PASSED). Real-data check used for this change: the raw OCR text of every ocr-debug_SKM_*.txt in Downloads through the real pipeline, before and after.
- Gap is not a warning (2026-10-19): a missing billing period is shown ONLY by the red gap row in the Bills table (app/utility-data.js row build, `detectGap`, computations/normalization.js). `period_gap` is deleted from computations/bill-flags.js (no flag, no count, no neighbor-row tint, not in the banner, meter pill, building row, project row or Review Bill Corrections). `tools/gate-single-bill-flag-count.js` fails if it returns to `BILL_COUNTED_FLAG_RULES`. The Bills banner (`#billFlagBanner`, app/utility-data.js `renderBillsPane`) has no preamble: one line per flagged bill, "<Month>: <reason>; <reason>", from `billFlagLine` (computations/bill-flags.js); the row hover is the same text (`billRowHoverText`). Reasons are short ("Missing kWh", "Lines add up to $X, bill prints $Y"). Check: `node tools/test-bill-flag-rules.js`, `node tools/gate-single-bill-flag-count.js`.

## Position-based bill reading (2026-10-19, branches 2026-10-19-position-reading and 2026-10-19-position-reading-p3)
- What: for a scanned bill page, the charge rows and the two page totals are found by WHERE they are printed, not only by what the OCR letters say (Matt: "If WALER sits exactly where WATER is on every other bill, it is WATER"). Layers: position finds the field (the label slot sets the label; the value comes from the amount column) -> OCR gives the characters -> a format regex validates -> the page sum check proves (`_lbg_pageTotalCheck`, app/energy-savings.js, unchanged).
- Where: `computations/bill-layout.js` (pure, no DOM; browser global `BILL_LAYOUT`, node `require`). Templates are data (`CITY_NEW`: reference render 1520 x 1970 px, 6 anchors, 5 column x-ranges, the charge-row band, the 6 label slots, the 2 totals, the 3 crop cells of the targeted-crop fallback). `wordsFromOcr(result)` is the ONE reader of tesseract.js word boxes (v5 `data.words`, v7 `data.blocks`). `matchTemplate` finds 2 or more FIXED anchors by fuzzy match (confusables folded, edit distance by label length) and fits ONE uniform scale and offset (scale from the page size when the anchors sit under 15% of the page apart; rejected when the scale is off by more than 30% or an anchor lands more than 3% of the page from its fit; a rotated canvas never fits, a landscape half-sheet scan of the same bill does). The two FLOATING anchors (Current Bill, Total Amount Due) are printed right under the last charge row, so they move up on a short page: they are located by their text and their x position after the fit, never used for the fit, and a total whose label is not found gets no value (never guessed from the fit). `readPageByTemplate` groups the label-column words into rows, sets each row's slot by fuzzy label (a noise label takes the one slot between its neighbours; WATER and WATER PROTECTION may repeat), reads the amount, previous-read, current-read and usage cells of the row (a word is on a row when half of the smaller height overlaps; a value cell takes only words with a digit or a bare sign, so a ruled line or a smudge read as letters leaves the cell empty), validates the amount with `MONEY_STRICT` (raw text kept when it fails, never a guess), and emits normalized lines ("4,000 4,500 500 WATER 100.00", "Current Bill $976.39"). `readBestPass(passes)` runs every template on every OCR pass of the page that has word boxes and keeps the read with the most format-valid values.
- Capture (app/bill-analysis.js, the page loop `_processOnePage`): every `allPassTexts[pgNum].push` keeps `words`, `w`, `h` beside `text` (6 sites: the two primary passes, the rotation winner, the two rotated re-passes, the Otsu pass). No extra OCR call; the boxes come with the text. Word boxes stay in memory only (`window._pdfOcrPasses`); `window._pdfPositionReads[pgNum]` holds the compact record (`debugRecord`: mode, template, pass, anchors, fit, rows with slot/label/value/ok, totals, lines) and is cleared on Clear with `_pdfOcrPasses`.
- Hand-off (same loop, just before the targeted-crop fallback): `await BILL_LAYOUT.readPage(allPassTexts[pgNum], _rereadBox)` (readBestPass, then the box re-read below); a match appends `linesBlock` to the page text behind `%%POSITION_READ%% ... %%POSITION_READ_END%%`, with one `%%POSITION_HELD%% <SLOT>:: <reason>` line BEFORE the block for every value cell the re-read could not prove; no match appends one `%%POSITION_READ_FALLBACK%% <reason>` line. The City reader (`_extractNew`, app/energy-savings.js) strips all three; when the block is present its lines are the ONE source of the commodity rows loop, `_lbg_readFuelAdj`, Total Amount Due and Current Bill (account, address, dates and balances still come from the page text); a held slot is null and HELD with the engine's reason (`gas.held` -> `_lbg_buildGasBill` returns the held bill before any reconcile; `_fuelAdjMeta.heldReason` -> `resolveFuelAdjOrHold` holds before any residual; every other slot through `_lbg_flagHeld` on every bill of the page). In position mode a Fuel Adjustment exists ONLY when the block has a FUEL ADJUSTMENT row (`fuelAdjLineSeen`), never inferred from the Gas row (2026-10-19: the Sep 29 scan p2 prints no Fuel Adjustment line; the text rule invented -309.67). Every bill of the page carries `_readMode` ('position' | 'text'), `_readFallbackReason`, and `_pageTotals` { currentBill, currentBillDerived, totalAmountDue, totalAmountDueHeld } (the two page totals as the reader decided them). The targeted-crop fallback (`_lbgNeedsCropFallback`) reads its three crop cells from `BILL_LAYOUT.CITY_NEW.cropCells`; it fires only when the page text (block included) still lacks the total or the Water line.
- Runs in the browser when a scanned bill PDF is read (upload queue, Review Bill Corrections scan, stored-bill repair pass through the page cache). No schedule, no timer, no network, no env var. Cost: under 5 ms per pass per page.
- Box re-read (phase 3, 2026-10-19; `rereadBoxes` in computations/bill-layout.js, the crop in app/bill-analysis.js `_rereadBox`): a value cell that fails the money format (or is ambiguous: two digit words whose rightmost is money on its own, pen digits left of the print), or a page whose rows do not add up to the printed Current Bill, is re-read from a crop of the page image: the page rendered once at 12x (`_renderPageHQCached(LBG_CROP_ZOOM)`, the render the targeted-crop fallback shares; released at the end of the page), rotated as the winning pass was (the `-rot90|180|270` suffix of the pass label), the cell cropped by the engine's box (pass pixels -> fractions, `_cropCanvasRegion`), scaled to the engine's zoom (`_scaleCanvas`), 8 px white margin (`_padCanvas`), grayscale, tesseract page mode 7, with the digit whitelist `BILL_LAYOUT.REREAD_WHITELIST` and without (`REREAD_CONFIGS`: 3 readings per cell, about 0.3 s each, failing cells only). Candidates (`candidatesFromText`): every valid-money token, a lost point placed before the last two digits, a leading `$` read as a digit dropped, both signs; the page's own raw reading counts too. The one reading taken is the candidate that makes the rows add up to the printed Current Bill (template `sumRule`, tolerance 0.01), fewest changes to format-valid cells first: stage 1 re-reads the failing cells, stage 2 (only when stage 1 cannot add up) re-reads every cell and lets at most two format-valid cells change ("52,719.54" beside a "4335"). A row the order rule placed from a noise label with no value may be no row at all (candidate ABSENT: dropped when the page adds up without it). No holding combination, or more than one: the failing cells are HELD with the reason (which row, what was read on the page and in the re-read). Template data says what may be re-read (`reread: { rows: true, totals: ['Current Bill'] }`); Total Amount Due has no sum proof on the page and is never re-read. Label bands taller than 1.5 row pitches (`rowPitch` 27.5 ref px; a pen line merged GAS and FUEL ADJUSTMENT into one tall OCR box) are split by the pitch; a row whose label is noise and that sits between two slotted rows takes the slot at its pitch distance; a label-column word under 0.3 pitch tall (a pen stroke read as "Co") is no label. Measured settings and the before/after counts: `_context/research/2026-10-19-ocr-accuracy-methods.md` (page mode 8 reads nothing; mode 7 at 10x reads 250 of 275 cells; Otsu and padding do not help). Total Amount Due check (ONE check, `_lbg_totalDueCheck`, app/energy-savings.js): when Current Bill is printed and the four balance lines add up to the printed Account Balance line, Total Amount Due must equal Current Bill plus that balance; a contradicted total is held with the reason (`_pageTotals.totalAmountDueHeld`), never stored. Not done yet: the Utility E meter table and page 1 by position; other providers.
- Utility E Billing Details page (phase 4, 2026-10-19, the 2026-10-19 phase-4 branch): template `UTILITY_E_BD` (computations/bill-layout.js; reference render 1530 x 1980 px, the 2.5x pass). The FIXED anchors are the page frame (Customer Name, Account Number, Billing Date at the top; the Contact Information Change Form lines at the bottom; two are enough); the Billing Details header is a REQUIRED floating anchor (a page without it, the bill's summary page, never matches: `matchTemplate` returns the reason); Subtotal, Current Charges and the meter table header ("Multiplier") float with the block. The row band runs from the header to the Current Charges label, else to the meter header (`chargeRows.stopAnchors`; the "Meter" word above it then opens a kind-less, value-less row that the re-read drops as ABSENT). `rowMode: 'list'` (`readListRows`): the label-column bands in the band are rows, not slots; a band that starts a kind (`kinds`, fuzzy at the band start by `kindOfBand`: a kind whose first word OCR lost still matches on its tail of 5 or more letters one edit worse, a run as close to two kinds such as "ECR Chg" is no kind) opens a row; a kind-less band continues the open row (the second line of a two-line label) unless that row already has a format-valid amount; the amount is the amount-cell words of the band that carries digits, a later valid amount replacing a garbled one; a band on a found Subtotal or Current Charges anchor row is that total by position; a kind-less row with a value between two known kinds takes the ONE kind between them in print order (`kindsByOrder`, the City order rule); rows under the Subtotal are `below`. Money format per template (`moneyFormat`: every Utility E amount prints with "$", so "$284.09-" or "15.88" fails the format and is re-read and proved, never taken as valid). TWO identities, proved one group at a time by the same box re-read (`sumGroups`, `rereadGroup`; `pageSum(read, tpl, values, group)`): group 'rows' = the charge rows add up to the Subtotal; group 'below' = the proved Subtotal plus the rows below it equals Current Charges. In list mode every row counts (kind known or not) and a kind-less row may be negative (a credit whose label OCR lost); a row of a known non-credit kind never (`sumRule.creditRows` = BillOffset, SolarCredit). A kind-less row with no value may be ABSENT (dropped when the group adds up without it). A Subtotal or Current Charges whose label OCR lost is found by structure (`findTotalsByStructure`: the first label-less valid amount equal to the sum of the two or more rows above it; the label-less valid amount under the Subtotal equal to Subtotal plus the rows between them); a total still not found is held with the reason ("no Subtotal box was found on the page"). The lines handed to the reader keep the "$" (`moneyPrefix`): "Customer Chg $102.86", "Energy Chg On Pk Win 7,283.8800 kWh at $0.04146 per kWh $301.99", "ECA Chg 04-01-2026-04-29-2026 for 52,879.6350 kWh at $0.01763 per kWh $932.27", "Bill offset -$863.35", "Subtotal $6,114.62", "Current Charges $6,114.62"; a held row's line has no amount. `debugRecord` rows carry `below`.
- Utility E reader (app/energy-savings.js, the extract function of the Utility E rule; same hand-off as the City reader): the `%%POSITION_READ%% template=utility-e-bd` block(s) in the bill's section are the ONE text (`tc`) every charge-line reader parses (xChg, xRate, the kWh tier readers, facKW / demKW / tdcKW, Tax exempt, Bill offset, Parallel Generation Credit, Franchise Fee, Sales Tax, Subtotal, Current Charges, the Off Pk Sum label repair, the detached-amount reader, the Customer Chg cents repair); the meter table, service dates, account, address and the front-page Miscellaneous lines still come from the page text with the markers stripped. TotalCurrentCharges comes ONLY from the block's "Current Charges" line: when the engine did not read that box the total is null and the bill is held with the engine's reason (`_positionHeld`, `_gateReasons`, `_gateTripped`, `_manualReview` / `_manualReviewLabel`; never the Subtotal, never a sum; the text-mode Subtotal fallback runs only without a block). A field named on a `%%POSITION_HELD%%` line is null and never derived (bill-analysis Strategy C fills only a field that has a value). Every Utility E bill carries `_readMode` ('position' | 'text') and `_readFallbackReason` (the engine's utility-e-bd reason). Fixed with it: xRate's next-line amount search took the amount of a line that starts another charge before testing CHG_STOP (a held On Pk Win line took the Off Pk Sum $38.35 and the two-part sum wrote 49.58); now only the text before that label may carry the amount.
- Check: `node tools/test-bill-layout.js` section L (16 Utility E checks on the committed synthetic pages). `node tools/gate-bill-ground-truth.js`: providers City and Utility E; synthetic pages of both always (`tools/synthetic-pages/bill-ground-truth-synthetic.json`, pages with `provider: 'utility-e'`, E1 to E8, built by the same make script from `UTILITY_E_BD`); with `BILL_GT_UTILITY_E_FILE` naming the local Utility E ground truth (the dated Utility E file in `_context/ground-truth/`: 35 real Billing Details pages read by eye, 20 fields each: 15 charge fields, Subtotal, Current Charges, the service period, printed Days) the real pages run both paths from the saved words in the same words folder and the re-read cache `<image>.png.rereads.json` (same `BILL_GT_OCR_MODULES` / `BILL_GT_LANG_PATH`); the gate loads the real `calcDays` (with `_fixISO` / `_parseISO`) from app/utility-data.js by source. 2026-10-19 result: before (origin/main 0fb2e0c9, text reader = production) 683 correct / 10 wrong / 2 held of 700 values; after (position path, every page matched, 396 crop re-reads) 691 / 1 / 3. The one wrong value is NumberOfDays on one page (the meter table's end read date OCR'd 07/08 for the printed 07/09, outside the Billing Details block, the same on both paths; the bill carries `_numberOfDaysCheck.printedDaysAgree: false`). The 3 held: two cells on a page whose details continue on a page outside the scan (no Subtotal printed, nothing to prove against) and one Subtotal whose label OCR lost beside a Bill offset read "Bill OSH $2,674.94" (no identity holds). City ground truth unchanged: 285 / 0 / 11.
- The proof of a re-read (`pageSum` / `sumHolds`, computations/bill-layout.js): the rows add up to the printed Current Bill in integer cents (no tolerance; a one-cent digit error is a failed sum and is re-read), and no row outside `sumRule.creditRows` (City: FUEL ADJUSTMENT) and no Current Bill is negative (a whole-page sign flip adds up too; it is never a proof). `rereadCrop(box, pass, oriented, zoom)` (same file) is the one rule for the crop geometry and zoom scale of the browser box re-read for every turn of the page (0/90/180/270; the scale is `zoom / (oriented.width / pass.w)`). In `_rereadBox` (app/bill-analysis.js) the turned 12x render is built once per page and released after `readPage`; a recognize timeout's `_replacementWorker` is adopted into `workerBox.current` as at every other recognize site.
- Check: `node tools/test-bill-layout.js` (synthetic word boxes only, 70 checks incl. the J re-read checks and the K proof-rule checks; in the regression gate's tools/test-*.js glob; expect "0 failed"). Ground-truth gate: `node tools/gate-bill-ground-truth.js` (in the regression gate's static list): runs the real City rule over committed SYNTHETIC pages (`tools/synthetic-pages/bill-ground-truth-synthetic.json`, built by `tools/synthetic-pages/make-bill-ground-truth-synthetic.js`; re-run it after a template change; the folder is named `synthetic-pages`, not `fixtures`, because the pre-push scan treats a `fixtures/` path as client data and the pages carry fake dollar figures) and, when `BILL_GT_FILE` names the local ground-truth file (`_context/ground-truth/2026-10-19-city-bill-pages.json`, real data, never in the repo) and the words folder exists (`BILL_GT_WORDS_DIR`, default the file's pagesDir, holding `<image>.png.ocr.json` and the re-read cache `<image>.png.rereads.json`), over the 37 real pages in both paths (position = production path for a matched page, text = fallback); fails on any WRONG value on the production path (a held value with a named reason is not a failure). A re-read missing from the cache is OCR'd only when `BILL_GT_OCR_MODULES` names a node_modules with tesseract.js and @napi-rs/canvas (and `BILL_GT_LANG_PATH` the eng.traineddata folder); otherwise it reads as "" and is counted as a cache miss. Without the real inputs (CI) the real part is skipped with a message. 2026-10-19 result: before (581242cf) 271 correct / 14 wrong / 11 held of 296 values; after 285 / 0 / 11. In the browser after a City PDF is read: `window._pdfPositionReads` lists every page with its mode, rows (`reread`, `absent`, `held` per row), `rereads` and `held`; a bill's `_readMode` is 'position' and `_readFallbackReason` is null; the page text in the debug file shows the `%%POSITION_READ%%` block (and any `%%POSITION_HELD%%` lines) at the end of each matched page.
- Utility E final hold re-check (app/energy-savings.js, end of the Utility E extractor, before `_printedTotalCheck`): the position engine's holds (`_positionHeld`, the same strings in `_gateReasons`) are re-checked against the FINAL values. A later step can fill a held field (the sum-gap fill of a Customer Chg whose dollars were read, "$48}"). When the final lines add up to the Subtotal to the cent (the Subtotal read on the page, else the printed Current Charges minus the printed lines below it) and Subtotal + those lines = the printed Current Charges to the cent, with no negative charge except Bill offset and the solar credit, the Subtotal "not read" hold and the hold of every field that now has a value are dropped, and the bill is not held if none is left. A field still empty keeps its hold; TotalCurrentCharges is never set by this step. Check: `node tools/test-bill-layout.js` section M (2 checks). Electric extracted-values panel (app/bill-analysis.js `_LAYOUT_ELECTRIC`): Commodity sits in Account Info; Previously Billed stays (read by Estimate missing period in app/csv-import.js as the gap period's actual cost).

## City bill repair rules and the account history check (2026-10-20, branch 2026-10-20-city-repair-rules)
- One rule fills or holds a value: a value fills when 2 or more independent sources agree (meter reads x the meter factor, charge / rate, printed duplicates, page identities, one-digit repair of the scan, the text layer). The hover names the method. A hold names the exact missing second source. Handwriting or scan noise alone never holds a value. The rule is in app/energy-savings.js (the shared autofix, one call site per field; the Total Amount Due fill is the sixth).
- Position engine (computations/bill-layout.js): (1) a cents digit lost ("4.0") gives ten candidates 4.00 to 4.09; the page sum keeps one or none. (2) A City dollar cell (the template flag `dollar: true` on Current Bill and Total Amount Due) always prints a leading "$": the first glyph read as 8, S, 5 or similar is dropped when the rest is a money figure, everything after the cents is noise ("(83,572.97 7)" = 3,572.97). (3) A cell the pen covers gets the page text-layer amount as one more candidate. (4) One empty box is the residual of the page sum (held with its missing second source named, other boxes still fill); two empty boxes hold ("one identity, two unknowns"). The Gas usage cell is dropped from the row when it fails the usage format, so it is never read as a meter read.
- Gas therms (app/energy-savings.js): when the position row has no usable usage cell, the reader keeps the first two numbers as the reads and holds the therms. The post-pass fills them from the meter factor, the printed Gas charge (rate check), and the usage cell as the text layer prints it ("1.214.164" = 1,214.164, 3 decimals; "1,086.18", 2 decimals). The raw read difference is never a source.
- Total Amount Due: when it contradicts Current Bill + balance carried, it is filled only when the page lines add up to the Current Bill (no derived Fuel Adjustment) AND the one-digit repair of the scan equals Current Bill + balance carried. Else it stays held with the missing source named. the page totals record carries both hover texts.
- Account history (app/energy-savings.js, the history table and its functions below the meter factor): for EVERY utility and account with saved bills (read from `utilityData`: bills with the same account number), a candidate is rejected when it is more than 10 x the largest or under 1/10 of the smallest saved value of that field. At least 3 saved values; totals always; a charge line only when the saved bills cover all 12 calendar months. The City Current Bill and Total Amount Due use the sum of the commodities of one printed statement (only complete statements). History only rejects; it never proposes a value and never counts as a source. It runs in the page read of computations/bill-layout.js (a reject callback that app/bill-analysis.js passes where it starts the page read) and in the shared autofix (`history`).
- Check: `node tools/test-city-repair-rules.js` (candidates, dollar cell, history rule, text-layer usage, page S9 with 0 holds); `node tools/gate-bill-ground-truth.js` (synthetic pages S6, S6b, S9; real pages with BILL_GT_FILE and BILL_GT_UTILITY_E_FILE; optional BILL_GT_HISTORY_FILE = a CompanyHub backup, so the history check runs on the real pages); `node tools/detect-history-rejects.js "<backup.json>" [--list]` (leave-one-out over a backup: each saved value is checked against the others; a flag marks bad saved data, it changes nothing). Run times are on demand, no schedule, no env var in the app. EXTRACTOR_RULES_VERSION is 4 (the stored-bill repair pass reads every bill again).
- 2026-10-20 result: City real pages, production path, before 285 / 0 / 11, after 287 / 0 / 9; Utility E 692 / 0 / 3 (unchanged). Text path unchanged (267 / 9 / 20).

## Gap text (2026-10-20, branch 2026-10-20-gap-compact; replaces the v144 chip)
- What: plain muted text, no badge or border. Building row (both lists): sub-line "2 meters · 2 of 2 baselined · [calendar icon] 1 bill gap". Meter pill: "39 bills · 1 gap". Projects table: "<phase> · N bill gaps". The left Projects nav shows no gap marker. It is NOT a warning: it is in no warning count, bell count or warning list.
- One rule: `computeMeterGaps(meter)` (computations/normalization.js) applies `detectGap` to each pair of bills next to each other in time (propane is skipped, as in the Bills table); `computeBuildingGaps` and `computeProjectGaps` roll it up. The text builder is `udGapTextHtml(gaps, goto, kind)` (kind bldg, pill or proj) (app/utility-data.js). Hover text: "30 days missing 08/04/2026 – 09/02/2026" (day count from `calcDays`, the same number as the Bills-tab "Gap in data" row). A click opens that meter's Bills tab and scrolls to the gap row (`udGoToGap`, `udGoToGapFromPill`, `udScrollToGapRow`; the row carries `data-gap-later`).
- Runs: on every redraw of those lists (no timer, no network). Needs no env var.
- Check: `node tools/test-gap-marker.js` (synthetic bills; runs in the regression gate by glob).
- Baseline flag (2026-10-20): `computeMeterGaps(meter, inBaseline)` (computations/normalization.js) sets `gap.baseline`; `computeBuildingGaps` and `computeProjectGaps` take an `isExcluded(meter)` predicate (callers pass `isBaselineExcluded(pid, m.id)`). `udGapTextHtml` lists baseline gaps first ("2 baseline gaps · 3 other gaps"; baseline = near-black semibold, other = muted). The Projects list shows ONLY baseline gaps; building rows, meter pills and the Bills tab show both. Building names in the Utility Data BUILDINGS list wrap (no ellipsis). Sidebar button `#siteDataBtn` says "Backup & Data" (the slim rail shows only its icon; the name is the hover text).

## Notifications and Sync status button (2026-10-20, branch 2026-10-20-gap-compact)
- What: the bell panel lists notifications. A bill import adds one per meter that got new periods, none when 0 were added. Text: title, "project · building · Commodity (utility)", "3 periods, Jun 2026 - Aug 2026", date and time. Clicking it closes the panel and opens that project, Utility Data tab, building, meter, Bills tab, scrolled to the first added period (`data-bill-start` on the row).
- One function builds the text: `billsImportedNotifText` (app/report-engine.js). One finds the link target: `notifTargetForMeter` (a notification with no target is not created). `notifyBillsImported` calls both; callers are `importBillCsvRows` and `importBillsMulti` (app/csv-import.js). The click calls `udGoToMeterBills` (app/utility-data.js), which uses `udGoToFlagged`. Stored in localStorage `ch_notifs`; entries from before this have no target: shown with no link, never deleted.
- Sync status button: `renderSyncStatusButton` (app/sync-ui.js) puts it in the sidebar `.sb-version` line: "v... . Sync status". A page with no such line (login page) keeps the old floating button. The sidebar has three modes (2026-10-21, see "Left sidebar mode" below). In the slim mode the version number is hidden and the Sync status button stays (small, two lines), so the button is always reachable. The archive link now sits at left:236px.
- Runs in the browser, no timer, no env var. Check: import a CSV on a meter, open the bell, click the entry.

- Utility E correction formulas (2026-10-20). A charge line is its own printed qty x its own printed rate; a date range on a line only names the part-period (app/energy-savings.js xRate: the date-range day fraction was removed; only the printed "(for N of Y days)" is read). Strategy B in app/bill-analysis.js _postExtractionVerify writes `_auto_corrected_<field>` only when `_rateFormulaOf` (app/bill-analysis.js, above _postExtractionVerify) finds every part's qty x rate equal to its charge to the cent; the reason text is that formula. A rate digit repair (app/energy-savings.js, the single-part and multi-part rate repair near the `_rate_repair_` writers) sets the record's computed and qty to the repaired values. The rate repair record is `_rate_repair_<field>` (ocrRate, derivedRate), no longer `_auto_corrected_rate_<field>`; the corrections banner (bill-analysis.js renderPDFFields) lists only records with a numeric corrected value. Check: `node tools/gate-correction-formulas.js` (synthetic pages; with BILL_GT_UTILITY_E_FILE also the real pages; in the regression gate static list). 2026-10-20 result on the 35 real Utility E pages, text path: before 4 pages with a record that is not true, after 0.
- Utility E prorated demand (2026-10-21, app/energy-savings.js). `_evgReadProration(block)` is the ONE reader of the printed "(for N of Y days)" on a kW charge line; it accepts "9 of 33days" (no space) and a cut Y (Y = null). `_evgResolveProrationDen(parts)` runs once per charge after its lines are read (no timer, no network): a part's Y is its own read, else the sum of the N of all parts, else the Y a sibling shows; a misread N is the shared Y minus the other parts' N. A value is used ONLY when the part's printed charge equals qty x rate x N / Y to the cent and the value read did not. A wrong printed charge matches no candidate and still warns (`_part_mismatches_<field>`). A part whose Y stays unknown is not prorated. Check: `node tools/test-utility-e-prorated-demand.js` (9 checks, in the regression gate by glob). 2026-10-21 sweep of the 2026-10-21 backup (953 stored bill pages through the Utility E reader): BilledKWCharge warnings 3 before, 0 after; no new warning.
- Utility E TDC date-range fraction as a check (2026-10-21, app/energy-savings.js). 28e78cc7 removed the date-range day fraction from `xRate`, and 25 correct bills got a false `_part_mismatches_TDCCharge` (printed charge = qty x rate x range days / bill days, to the cent). `xRate` now keeps the range days and bill days (`numDays`) on a kW line that has a date range and no printed fraction (`rangeNum`, `rangeDen`). `_evgResolveProrationDen` (the same function that reads the printed fraction) uses them first: only when the printed charge equals qty x rate x N / Y to the cent (Y = bill days, else the sum of the range days of the lines) and plain qty x rate does not. The part then holds the fraction, so the check passes. A charge matching neither keeps its warning. The stored qty and rate stay as printed, and `_rateFormulaOf` (Strategy B) is unchanged, so no corrected value comes from a fraction. Runs in the browser on bill extraction, no timer, no env var. Check: `node tools/test-utility-e-prorated-demand.js` (TDC cases at the end, runs in the regression gate).
- Layout field formats and page identities (2026-10-20). Field formats are data: `fieldFormats` on the City and Utility E templates and on three format-only layouts (gas utility, second city, gas supplier) in computations/bill-layout.js, read through the exported `fieldFormat(layout, field)`; each fact was checked on 3 or more real pages (decimals, digit counts, credit sign). The one fill-or-hold rule in app/energy-savings.js (the autofix, `format` argument) drops every one-digit repair of a scanned figure that breaks the field's format and takes no negative value for a never-negative field; callers pass the format from the layout. Page identities now feed that same rule: gas utility Mcf (reads / 10 x meter constant, WNA and Cost of Gas = Mcf x rate to the cent) and Total Amount Due (Previous Balance - Payments + Total Current Charges); second city Water or Sewer line lost its charge (Amount Due Now minus the other lines, plus the 6% franchise fee test); gas supplier site Trigger and Index lines ((Mmbtu + Fuel) x rate, Sub-Total minus the other line). They run at the end of each reader's extractAll (gas supplier: after the site list is built), on every read, no schedule, no env var. A value fills only when 2 or more sources agree; else the bill is held with a reason that names the missing second source. Electric Tax Exempt = -Bill offset stays as before (one side fills the other, cost-neutral); two reads that differ are now recorded in `_mirror_conflict`. EXTRACTOR_RULES_VERSION is unchanged (4). The ground-truth gate sets the layout object in its vm (`sandbox.BILL_LAYOUT`); the page loads computations/bill-layout.js before the readers use it, and a missing object means no format filter. Check: `node tools/test-layout-facts.js` (synthetic accounts, 59 checks, in the regression gate's tools/test-*.js glob, expect ALL PASSED); `node tools/gate-bill-ground-truth.js` with the real inputs: City 287 / 0 / 9 and Utility E 692 / 0 / 3, same before and after. Corrected 2026-10-21 against 23 real scanned pages (gas utility 7, second city 6, gas supplier 2, gas marketer 3, propane 5; the real pages stay outside the repo): a gas utility credit prints with a "CR" suffix (never a minus) and a payment prints "569.28CR" (stored positive); the second city prints no "$" and a trailing-minus fuel adjustment (".57-", no leading zero, now read); the gas supplier prints rates with 4 decimals and "$"; dial reads print 1 to 4 digits (no leading zero). Format facts now also carry `glyph` ('$', 'none', 'first'), `suffix` and the credit kinds 'lead', 'trail', 'CR'. Gas utility Total Amount Due is read from the "Total Amount Due" line (charges block); only when that line is lost, a bare "Amount Due" that is not "Amount Due After Due Date"; never a date ("Current Charges Due 03-10-26"). New fills, all through the one rule: gas utility Franchise Fee line (Total Current Charges minus every other line + a one-digit repair of the scan); second city Water franchise fee (6% of charge + debt lines, exact to the cent, 7 of 7 real lines, + a one-digit repair); the identities also run when a gas utility text holds one bill. Second city: Water debt is the sum of the Meter Debt lines (one per water meter); a lost or unreadable Meter Debt line holds the debt and bill total with a reason (never a guess). Gas supplier: a site whose Index or Trigger row label printed but whose amount was not read, and whose Sub-Total amount was not read, holds the site charge (`_wreChargeRowLost`) instead of showing the other row alone. The second city after-due-date amount (about 1.10 x, off by 1 to 2 cents) is not a source and is not used. Check on real pages (local only): run the readers on the OCR word boxes of the 2026-10-21 ground-truth files; result 168 correct, 0 wrong, 73 held with a named missing source. Second city account number (app/energy-savings.js `_lbg_decideAccount`, `_lbg_savedAccounts`, used in the second city `_extractPage`): the number fills only when 2 sources agree: the stub and the top box print the same number, or one printed read is a saved account number of that utility (`utilityData`). One printed read alone is held (`_accountHeld` names the missing source; the page is then keyed by service address like a page with no number). History never fills alone. Cause of the old wrong value: the first matching print was taken as the number (one OCR digit error became the key). Other utilities' account numbers are not on this path (Utility E and City read theirs through other readers; gas utility and gas supplier read from labelled fields). Skipped as not proven: the gas marketer and propane layouts have no layout facts here (no template), the gas supplier special weather credit sign (never seen), any "$" glyph rule for the gas utility, second city and gas supplier layouts was recorded as data only (nothing parses by it), the invoice-total identity of the gas supplier (fee and tax lines are not read), the usual-value test source for fixed lines, the late-fee ratios, and the City fuel-adjustment factor.

## kv_history on every write (2026-10-21)
- What: kv-sync.js `handlePut` (snapshot call at kv-sync.js:335, `shouldSnapshot` :380, `snapshotHistory` :387, `enforceHistoryRetention` after). Every PUT that changes a key (normal, tombstone, explicit overwrite) writes the previous row (key, value, version, updated_by, replaced_at) to `kv_history` BEFORE the PATCH. No row when the new value equals the old (hash compare), none for a new key, none for a stale-version PUT (409). One row per key+version (looked up first). If the history insert fails the PUT returns 502 and the row is not changed. PostgREST has no multi-statement transaction, so this is snapshot-then-CAS; a lost CAS leaves at most one extra row for the version still current.
- Why: on 2026-10-21 three keys were rewritten by normal PUTs with no saved old copy.
- Retention (free tier DB 500 MB): 20 rows per key; 5 rows per key when the value is over 1 MB (largest key about 9 MB, so at most about 45 MB); nothing older than 30 days. The "over 1 MB" test uses UTF-8 bytes (`Buffer.byteLength`), not characters. The trim (2-3 Supabase requests) runs only in a request that inserted a history row; a write with no snapshot (new key, equal value, snapshot row already there) makes no trim request, to save Netlify compute. No schema change and no migration (kv_history columns are unchanged).
- Check: `node test-kv-sync-history.js` (in-memory fake, no network). Runs in the regression gate. Restore: read the row in `kv_history` in the Supabase dashboard (newest `replaced_at` first).
- Needs: env vars SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY (unchanged).

## Project card headline quarter (2026-10-21, branch 2026-10-21-q-headline)
`nextQuarterToPresent(projId)` (computations/savings.js) picks the quarter the project card shows: the first quarter after the newest presented record (`getPresentedRecords`), else the latest billed quarter, never after the newest bill month. The "Bills through" date and the complete test skip delivery commodities (`DELIVERY_COMMODITIES`, app/core.js, currently Propane) because a propane delivery date is not a monthly bill date. Runs on each project dashboard render (app/core.js `initDashboardTab`). Needs no env var. Check: with Q1 and Q2 presented and bills to September, the card reads "Q3 2026 Total Savings"; run `node tools/test-next-quarter-to-present.js`.

## Left sidebar mode (2026-10-21)

- One shared control, `sidebar-mode.js` (repo root, `window.SidebarMode`), loaded in the `<head>` of energy-department.html and ems-leads.html (index.html is the sign-in page and has no sidebar). CSS is in `sidebar-rail.css`. It runs in the browser only: no timer, no schedule, no server call of its own, no env var.
- Three modes, pinned by the user: `expanded` (full sidebar, 220 px; 184 px under 1600 px), `slim` (rail 56 px wide, icon only, the name is the hover text, a click does what the full item does), `collapsed` (sidebar hidden, the page uses the full width). The control is one pin button at the top edge of the sidebar (`#sbPinBtn`) with a 3-line menu (`#sbPinMenu`: Open, Slim (1 icon wide), Collapsed (opens on hover)); the current mode has a check mark. In `slim` the pin is at the top of the rail. In `collapsed` a 10 px strip (`.sb-edge`) at the left edge of the page opens the sidebar over the page while the pointer is on it, so the pin stays reachable.
- State: html attribute `data-sb-mode`, set before the first paint (the script is a blocking script in `<head>`). Saved in `ch_sidebar_mode` (RAW_PER_USER_LOCAL_KEYS, app/db.js, so a user switch clears it): a localStorage copy for the first paint and a `DB.set` copy (per-user sync key, `ch_` rule in app/sync-classification.js) so it follows the user to another browser. When the DB is ready (`dbReady`), the DB copy wins. No saved choice: expanded at 900 px and wider, collapsed below 900 px (this replaces the old "Menu" button drawer, removed from energy-department.html and `buildMobileSidebarToggle` in app/site-functions.js; ems-leads no longer hides the sidebar under 900 px). The old unused key `ch_sidebar_collapsed` is gone.
- A sidebar item opts in with `data-sb-icon="<name>"` (names: `ICONS` in sidebar-mode.js, inline SVG, no emoji). The script puts the icon in `.s-ico` and wraps the label in `.s-txt`. A new sidebar item needs `data-sb-icon` or it has no icon in the slim rail.
- Check: open energy-department.html, click the pin, pick each mode, reload, open ems-leads.html: the mode stays. In DevTools: `localStorage.getItem('ch_sidebar_mode')` and `document.documentElement.dataset.sbMode`. Test: `node tools/test-gap-marker.js` (sidebar button label markup).

- `renderPDFFields` (app/bill-analysis.js, `buildCell`, `_roBox`, `_partCell`, the Data analysis bar) sends every OCR value, field key, label, hover reason and bar link through `_escHtml` (lib/formatting.js). A value inside an inline handler (`onclick`) goes through `_jsArg` (a JSON string, then `_escHtml`). No env var; runs in the browser on each render.
- `initDashboardTab` (app/core.js) has no date-based fallback: when `nextQuarterToPresent` returns null (no bills) the card shows no headline quarter and no quarter sums.

## Sync status: one place (2026-10-22)
- The bottom-right "N unsynced changes" pill is gone. The sidebar "Sync status" link (`#ch-sync-status-btn`, app/sync-ui.js `renderSyncStatusButton`) is the only place. `syncStatusView()` is the one function that computes its text and color from `_syncState` (unsynced count from `syncQueueChanged`/`dbReady`, offline from `dbOfflineBanner`, sign-in refused from `dbAuthRejected`; `dbHydrated` clears offline and refused). States: "Sync status", "Sync status (N unsynced)", "Sync status (offline)", "Sync status (sign-in refused)". Browser only, no env var.
